_source line 377 · 2026-05-22T23:23:44.564Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested rerunning the Gemma-2-27B Phase 2 Qwen Cross-Scoring experiment (exp_id: seed_gemma27b_c4_s42) on server cqa1-22115. Phase 1 (self-scoring) had already completed successfully with K*=2 and 1v2 AUC=0.6196, but Phase 2 failed because the original command included `--seed 42` which is not a valid argument for `run_pipeline.py`. The task was to rerun Phase 2 without the `--seed` parameter, using Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct as the cross-scorer model on cuda:0, and report the Qwen-scored pairwise AUC results.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Cross-model scoring (Phase 2): Using a different model (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct) to score features extracted from text generated by Gemma-2-27B
   - Pairwise AUC: Metric measuring distinguishability between adjacent generative depths (0v1, 1v2, etc.)
   - K* (saturation depth): Maximum depth where AUC > 0.60
   - Feature extraction pipeline: Surprisal computation + VocabDiv computation per depth level
   - `--skip_generation` flag: Skips text generation (Steps 2-3), only re-extracts features and runs analysis (Steps 4-5)
   - Symlinked data directories: Pattern used to share depth files between self-scored and cross-scored experiments without duplication
   - conda environment activation: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
   - AutoDL server with NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPUs (97GB each)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/run_pipeline.py`
     - The main pipeline script. Contains argparse with these arguments (line 621+): `--model_path`, `--data_dir`, `--results_dir`, `--num_samples` (default 5000), `--max_depth` (default 5), `--batch_size`, `--feat_batch_size`, `--top_p`, `--temperature`, `--do_sample`/`--no_sample`, `--generation_mode`, `--skip_generation`. Notably does NOT have a `--seed` argument.
     - Pipeline flow: Load model → (skip generation if flagged) → Step 4: Feature Extraction → Step 5: Analysis → Output results

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/run_gemma27b_c4.sh`
     - Original bash script for the full Gemma-27B experiment (Phase 1 + Phase 2). Both phases had `--seed 42` which caused Phase 2 to fail.
     - Phase 1 used `--model_path "/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/google/gemma-2-27b"` with `--generation_mode continuation`
     - Phase 2 used `--model_path "/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-1___5B-Instruct"` with `--skip_generation`

   - `/tmp/gemma27b_phase2_cmd.sh`
     - The failed Phase 2 retry script that also had `--seed 42` and `rm -f data/exp_gemma27b_c4/features.csv`

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_gemma27b_c4/`
     - Contains Phase 1 outputs: depth_0.jsonl through depth_5.jsonl (generated text at each depth), features.csv (self-scored features)

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/results/exp_gemma27b_c4/`
     - Contains Phase 1 self-scored results: pairwise_auc.json, jsd.json, ordinal_results.json, feature_importance.json, summary.txt, run.log
     - Self-scored AUC: 0v1=0.9695, 1v2=0.6196, 2v3=0.5388, 3v4=0.5069, 4v5=0.5124, K*=2

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_gemma27b_c4_qwen_scorer/`
     - Created by me with symlinks to the original depth files. This preserves original self-scored results.

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/results/exp_gemma27b_c4_qwen_scorer/`
     - Created by me for Phase 2 cross-scored output. Currently empty (job still running).

   - `/tmp/gemma27b_phase2_qwen.log`
     - Log file for the current Phase 2 run.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Original Phase 2 failure (`--seed 42` not recognized)**:
     - The error was: `run_pipeline.py: error: unrecognized arguments: --seed 42`
     - Fix: Removed `--seed 42` from the command entirely
   - **`src/run_pipeline.py` not found**:
     - The task description referenced `src/run_pipeline.py` but the actual path is `scripts/run_pipeline.py`
     - Fixed by searching with `find` command
   - **`output/` directory not found**:
     - Task referenced `output/` directory but data is in `data/` and results in `results/`
     - Found the correct paths via `find` and `ls`
   - **Preserving self-scored results**:
     - The failed Phase 2 script (`/tmp/gemma27b_phase2_cmd.sh`) was set to overwrite the same data/results dirs and delete features.csv
     - Fix: Created separate dirs (`exp_gemma27b_c4_qwen_scorer`) with symlinks, following the pattern used by other cross-scorer experiments (olmo_c4_qwen_scorer, pythia_c4_qwen_scorer)
   - **ssh_read_only pipe restriction**: Commands with `|` were blocked by the read-only tool. Used grep directly without pipes.
   - **SSH timeout on nohup**: The ssh_execute command timed out at 30s but the job launched successfully (PID captured from stdout).

5. Problem Solving:
   - Identified the root cause of Phase 2 failure (unsupported `--seed` argument)
   - Discovered the correct pipeline script location and its argparse interface
   - Established the cross-scorer directory pattern by examining existing successful cross-scorer experiments (symlinked depth files)
   - Successfully launched Phase 2 with corrected command (no --seed, separate output dirs)
   - Job is currently running on PID 78499, processing feature extraction across 6 depth levels (~2.7-3 min per depth)

6. All user messages:
   - First message: Detailed task description for rerunning Gemma-2-27B Phase 2 Qwen Cross-Scoring, including background, server info, step-by-step instructions, and expected outputs

   - Report Qwen-scored pairwise AUC (0v1, 1v2, 2v3) and K* determination
   - Verify self-scored results in `results/exp_gemma27b_c4/` are preserved
   - Optionally register the artifact

8. Current Work:
   Phase 2 job was launched and is actively running on server cqa1-22115, PID 78499. The job uses Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct to extract features from the Gemma-27B generated depth chain (depth_0 through depth_5, 5000 samples each). 

   The command launched was:
   ```bash
   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination && export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache && export HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 && export TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 && export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 && nohup python3 scripts/run_pipeline.py --model_path "/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2___5-1___5B-Instruct" --data_dir "data/exp_gemma27b_c4_qwen_scorer" --results_dir "results/exp_gemma27b_c4_qwen_scorer" --skip_generation --num_samples 5000 --max_depth 5 > /tmp/gemma27b_phase2_qwen.log 2>&1 &
   ```

   Progress observed from log:
   - Depth 0: started 07:10:42, completed ~07:13:32
   - Depth 1: started 07:13:32, completed ~07:16:16
   - Depth 2: started 07:16:16 (last seen in logs)
